  > "to avoid falling into a 'business-  as-  usual' mentality whereby business is  undertaken  day-  to-  day  without  any  thought  for  the  medium-  term impact or wider strategic picture"
  > "adopting a 'Deposits Analysis Template' approach to ensure regular review and update, to be 'owned' by ALCO and delegated to Treasury or Finance."
  > — Chapter 23 CS6, line 13034 ^choudhry-cs-23

- Case study 7 (ngân hàng khu vực nhỏ ở EU, rà soát chiến lược năm 2014): mô hình bán lẻ và doanh nghiệp nhỏ, RWA chủ yếu là cho vay thế chấp nhà ở rồi đến cho vay chính quyền địa phương; ràng buộc rất chặt về cả vốn và thanh khoản nên không thể đổi hướng hay tăng tài sản đáng kể nếu chủ sở hữu không bơm vốn.
  > "we present a report written by the author after a strategy review of a small regional EU bank in 2014, and presented to the bank's Board. This bank had a conventional retail and small corporate business model, and its risk-  weighted assets were overwhelmingly residential mortgages followed by local authority lending. However, it suffered from extreme constraints in both capital and liquidity, which restricted any  material  change  in  direction,  or  asset  growth,  unless  there  was  new equity injection from the owner."
  > — Chapter 23 CS7, line 13042 ^choudhry-cs-24

- Bài học CS7: HĐQT phải rõ ngân hàng muốn trở thành gì trong trung hạn (hình dạng, cấu trúc, quy mô, mục tiêu franchise, khẩu vị rủi ro); giao dịch ad hoc không thay được chiến lược và mô hình kinh doanh nhất quán; ngân hàng nhỏ ít hiện diện trên thị trường bán buôn hiếm khi triển khai thành công chiến lược Trading Book mới với lợi nhuận trên vốn bền vững.
  > "It  is  important for the Board of a bank to have a clear idea of what exactly  they  want  the  bank  to  be,  its  shape,  structure,  size,  franchise objectives  and  risk  appetite,  in  the  medium  term,  and  work  towards achieving that goal."
  > "Beware of 'quick fixes' which are generally never quick and rarely a fix; ad hoc transactions are no substitute for a coherent, articulated strategy and business model."
  > "It is rare for a small bank with little or no presence in wholesale markets to implement a successful new Trading Book strategy and generate acceptable sustained return on capital for the risk incurred"
  > — Chapter 23 CS7, line 13048 ^choudhry-cs-25

- Nhận xét kết của tác giả: không có giải pháp 'one size fits all'; template chỉ là điểm xuất phát; FTP là ví dụ về một mảng được các ngân hàng xử lý theo nhiều cách khác nhau.
  > "A good example is internal funds transfer pricing, which is handled in many different ways by banks."
  > "There is no 'one size fits all' in finance and the best solutions to problems arising in bank management are those that are best suited to the organisation in question. A template approach to any issue arising"
  > "is only good as the starting point."
  > — Chapter 23 Final Remarks, line 13058 ^choudhry-cs-26

- Theo tác giả, ALCO là diễn đàn tốt nhất để tranh luận và thống nhất hướng xử lý vì có đủ thành phần rủi ro và lợi nhuận; cần một người hoặc nhóm chủ động phát hiện và đưa vấn đề lên ban điều hành.
  > "This is why the bank's ALCO is the best forum to debate issues and agree  courses  of  action,  although  there  are  numerous  other  committees in a bank as well. It's just that ALCO contains the right mix of risk and reward around the table."
  > "Without  such  a  person  or  team,  a  bank  can  sleepwalk  into  trouble  without knowing it."
  > — Chapter 23 Final Remarks, line 13062 ^choudhry-cs-27

- Câu hỏi ôn tập CS5 liệt kê năm nguồn rủi ro chính của IRRBB (gap, yield curve, basis, run-off, option) và yêu cầu phân biệt phương pháp quản trị IRRBB dùng phái sinh và không dùng phái sinh.
  > "Describe and discuss the following primary sources of risks with respect to interest-rate risks in a banking book:"
  > "Distinguish between derivative based methods and non-  derivative based methods of managing IRRBB and comment on the appropriateness  of  such  methods  given  the  circumstances  of  a  particular bank."
  > — Appendix 23.1 CS5, line 13105 ^choudhry-cs-28

- Chương 24 trích sách *The Country Banker* (1890) của George Rae để cho thấy nguyên tắc cốt lõi của ngân hàng ít thay đổi; Rae viết trong thời kỳ chưa có cơ quan quản lý, yêu cầu vốn tối thiểu hay giám sát.
  > "We demonstrate that key principles of banking are timeless, with reference to a work published in the UK in the 19th century."
  > "This book was published in 1890, with the title The Country Banker: His Clients, Cares and Work ."
  > "he was writing during an era when there was no regulatory authority, no minimum capital requirement, no supervision process and no SREP visits."
  > — Chapter 24, line 13127 ^choudhry-rae-1

- Rae đặt dự trữ sẵn sàng sử dụng bằng một phần ba nợ với công chúng; ngân hàng tỉnh nắm bình quân 24%, ngân hàng khác 36%; mức dự trữ phụ thuộc tính chất tiền gửi, và phải đủ cho cả rút tiền gửi lẫn nhu cầu chiết khấu, cho vay biến động.
  > "THE RESERVE. To guard against all probable demands, therefore, I  have  put  your  immediately available resources - your financial reserve - at one-  third the amount of your liabilities to the public."
  > "The range of usage in this matter is very great, and is governed, in some measure, by the character of the deposits held."
  > "Country banks, like yourselves. . .hold average reserves of 24 per cent. Others. . .have reserves averaging 36 per cent."
  > "Your reserve, therefore, should always be sufficient to meet every description of demand upon it."
  > — Chapter 24 Banking Finance, line 13179 ^choudhry-rae-2

- Rae: ngân hàng phải hoàn trả người gửi tiền ngay khi được yêu cầu, trong khi người vay không chịu nghĩa vụ tương tự.
  > "whereas the banker is bound to refund the money to every depositor the moment it is asked for, the persons to whom he has lent it do not feel themselves under any such peremptory obligation."
  > "He must pay his debts, the moment they are due and demanded, or close his doors."
  > — Chapter 24 Deposits and Runs, line 13197 ^choudhry-rae-3

- Rae: lãi suất tiền gửi phụ thuộc chủ yếu điều kiện gửi; tiền gửi rút theo yêu cầu phải đầu tư vào chứng khoán chất lượng cao nhất nên lợi suất thấp; tiền gửi báo trước cho phép đầu tư vào tài sản kém thanh khoản hơn, lợi suất cao hơn; ví dụ 2% (không kỳ hạn), 2¼% (báo trước thông thường), 2½% (kỳ hạn dài hơn).
  > "The [interest] rate  thus  agreed  upon will vary in accordance, partly with the custom of localities, partly with the usage of individual banks; but mainly with the condition upon which the money is deposited."
  > "because deposits payable on demand must be invested in banking securities of the highest class, and will consequently yield a low rate of return."
  > "Deposits, on the other hand, which are subject to notice, afford you time to turn in, and could be employed in less available [less liquid] securities and bringing you a somewhat better yield of interest. If your rate for deposits payable on demand were 2 per cent for example, it might be 2¼ on those subject to your usual term of notice, and 2½ on those lodged for longer terms."
  > — Chapter 24 Interest and Discount, line 13205 ^choudhry-rae-4

- Template chính sách và báo cáo tài chính trên website đi kèm sách dùng ngân hàng giả định 'ChoudWest Bank', ngân hàng thương mại thông thường với bảng cân đối GBP 38 tỷ.
  > "Note that the hypothetical and completely fictitious bank 'ChoudWest Bank' is used as the name of the bank for whom template policy statements and financial statements are provided. ChoudWest Bank is a plain vanilla commercial bank of GBP 38 billion balance sheet size."
  > — Chapter 25, line 13275 ^choudhry-web-1

- Thư mục Chương 7 (IRRBB) trên website gồm: slide IRRBB (2 phần), template chính sách IRRBB, mô hình IRRBB Excel, báo cáo gap lãi suất UK FSA017 thực tế tại 31/03/2020, slide NMD và IRRBB, slide CSRBB. Chương 23 có báo cáo rà soát IRRBB của ngân hàng thương mại.
  > "Word document: IRRBB Policy template"
  > "Excel file: IRRBB model."
  > "Excel file: UK FSA017 interest rate gap report regulatory return, actual example, as at 31 March 2020"
  > "PowerPoint slides: Non-  maturing deposits and IRRBB"
  > "PowerPoint slides: Credit spread risk in the banking book"
  > "Word document: Commercial bank IRRBB review and report"
  > — Chapter 25, line 13378 ^choudhry-web-2

Ghi chú: dòng 13277–13283 của raw chứa thông tin đăng nhập website; không trích.

### Unit u10 — Ch5 Strategic ALM (phần cuối: ALM optimisation, lãi suất âm) & Ch6 Balance Sheet Management (FX, cross-currency swap, funding short/long, interest-rate futures)

- Quy trình triển khai Strategic ALM gồm bốn bước: ALCO có thẩm quyền điều hành lập ngân sách; Board RAS xác định KRI/KPI cần tối ưu; lập kế hoạch tích hợp, lặp lại tới khi KRI/KPI tối ưu; trình ALCO và Board phê duyệt.
  > "Ensure ALCO has the requisite authority to have effective control and direction of the strategic and annual budget setting process."
  > "Ensure the Board RAS is in place that identifies the key risk indicators (KRIs) and key performance indicators (KPIs) that the bank wishes to optimise."
  > "Send this annual plan and new strategic plan for ALCO and Board approval."
  > — Chapter 5, line 4780 ^choudhry-salm-1

- Vai trò ALM đã chuyển từ cơ chế phản ứng, tập trung immunisation sổ ngân hàng, sang bảo đảm ngân hàng có lãi và giữ NIM.
  > "The role of ALM used to be seen as a reactive mechanism to adjust the risk positions generated by business units. It was focused on immunisation of the banking book rather than proactive management of balance sheet risks and optimisation of the banking book composition."
  > "Within the ALM unit the remit now is to ensure that the bank remains profitable, and that the ALM techniques used seek to preserve NIM."
  > — Chapter 5, line 4795 ^choudhry-opt-1

- Ví dụ ALM optimisation có mục tiêu tối thiểu hoá tổng chi phí vốn bằng cách chọn tỷ trọng các nguồn vốn, chịu hạn mức nội bộ và quy định về thanh khoản, funding và rủi ro lãi suất.
  > "We will illustrate the application of an optimisation technique in the banking book with reference to a specific objective of minimising the overall cost of funding of a hypothetical commercial bank. The goal in this illustration is to determine the share of different funding sources so that the overall cost is as low as possible."
  > "For instance, there will be internal and regulatory limits for liquidity, funding and acceptable exposure of interest-rate risk."
  > — Chapter 5, line 4799 ^choudhry-opt-2

- Hàm mục tiêu và ràng buộc tuyến tính thì dùng linear programming (simplex); phi tuyến thì dùng kỹ thuật khác như Lagrange multiplier.
  > "If the objective and constraints functions are linear, we can apply a method known as the linear programming problem (LPP). Algorithm simplex is commonly used for solving the problems of linear programming, and can be solved with Matlab or Solver in an Excel spreadsheet. However, when the objective and constraint functions are not linear other techniques should be used."
  > — Chapter 5, line 4801 ^choudhry-opt-3

- Phần lớn ràng buộc là bất đẳng thức, ví dụ Survival Days yêu cầu HQLA đủ bù dòng ra ròng trong stress tối thiểu (ví dụ) 90 ngày; khi đó cần phương pháp như interior-point (Matlab `fmincon`).
  > "Another important point when dealing with optimisation of the banking book is that most of the constraints imposed on the model are inequality functions rather than equality ones."
  > "requires that there is a sufficient amount of high-quality liquid assets (HQLA) in place to cover net funding outflows in a stress event such that the bank is able to survive for a minimum of (say) 90 days."
  > "An example might be the application of the optimisation method called the interior- point algorithm based on the Lagrange multipliers method, which is embedded in the Matlab solver: fmincon ."
  > — Chapter 5, line 4805 ^choudhry-opt-4

- Ví dụ ChoudWest Bank (giả định): cấu trúc funding mục tiêu tính bằng solver phi tuyến tiết kiệm 0,6 tỷ USD mỗi năm.
  > "The examined problem has been set up in the form of non- linear objective functions which minimise the cost of funds. In this way, the target funding structure has been calculated, which leads to the … 0.6 billion of savings on an annual basis for the ChoudWest Bank."
  > — Chapter 5, line 4807 ^choudhry-opt-5

- Bảng cân đối mục tiêu có lợi từ quản lý tích hợp thanh khoản và rủi ro lãi suất, trái với quản lý silo vẫn phổ biến nhất; chỉ số IRRBB và thanh khoản được giữ ở mức mong muốn.
  > "We show that the target balance sheet structure benefits from the integrated and proactive management of liquidity and interest-rate risk under one holistic approach."
  > "This is as opposed to the silo management of liquidity and IRRBB, still the most common approach to ALM risk management applied in banks. The IRRBB and liquidity metrics, as a result of the exercise, are optimised as they drive the desired funding structure to keep risk metrics at the desirable level (not too high and not too low)."
  > — Chapter 5, line 4809 ^choudhry-opt-6

- ECB áp lãi suất cơ bản âm từ tháng 6/2014; ngày 6/7/2020 toàn bộ đường cong trái phiếu Đức và EUR OIS nằm dưới 0%.
  > "Then in June 2014 the European Central Bank introduced its negative base rate policy for the eurozone, and so it has remained up to the time of this book's publication. Figure 5.13 shows the German sovereign bond curve and the euro OIS swap curve as at 6 July 2020. We see that the entire yield curve is below 0.00%, out to 30- year and 40- year tenors."
  > — Chapter 5, line 4817 ^choudhry-neg-1

- Khi lãi suất âm, chính sách nên rà soát trước khi phát sinh khoản vay/tiền gửi: hợp đồng vay thả nổi ghi rõ sàn 0,00%; điều khoản tiền gửi cho nhóm khách hàng không chịu lãi âm (bán lẻ eurozone phần lớn có sàn 0,00%); phí vận hành cho tài khoản thanh toán.
  > "In an environment of negative interest rates, ALM discipline is still concerned with the same issues - that of managing liquidity and interest-rate risks and seeking to preserve and maximise NIM."
  > "loan documentation should be reviewed to ensure that it states clearly that loan rates will be floored at 0.00% with no possibility of going below that rate for floating- rate loans;"
  > "example, in the eurozone retail customer deposit rates are in most cases floored at 0.00%, or floored at the very least for deposit balances up to a specified maximum level. On the other hand, some private bank customer deposit balances and corporate customer balances may experience a negative rate;"
  > "for current account balances and other operational deposits a bank may choose to charge an operating fee to the customer, which will cover the negative interest rate that the bank itself is suffering when it places deposits with the central bank."
  > — Chapter 5, line 4831 ^choudhry-neg-2

- Xử lý NMD khi lãi suất âm: tiền gửi bán lẻ và SME có sàn 0,00% được coi như sản phẩm "fixed-rate"; khi đó có thể vào IRS nhận cố định, trả thả nổi, tenor bằng behavioural tenor của NMD; nếu có, dùng balance guarantee swap có notional điều chỉnh theo số dư NMD.
  > "for large corporate and certain large balance retail customer deposits, the bank may pass on the negative interest rate. Retail and SME deposits are generally floored at 0.00% and henceforth treated as a 'fixed- rate' product;"
  > "viewing NMDs as fixed rate means one can enter into an IRS for the balance to receive fixed- rate and pay floating- rate, with the IRS tenor matching the behavioural tenor of the NMDs;"
  > "if available the IRS may be a 'balance guarantee' swap which adjusts the notional value as the NMDs balance changes, which is more effective in responding to customer behaviour;"
  > — Chapter 5, line 4843 ^choudhry-neg-3

- Hệ quả hành vi khác của lãi suất âm: huy động có kỳ hạn đắt hơn và ảnh hưởng NIM; khách hàng có xu hướng roll over khoản vay; cho mục đích IRR, khoản vay và tiền gửi thả nổi có thể xếp vào bucket 1 tháng.
  > "it is relatively more expensive to raise term deposits, because there is little incentive for customers to lock away money for longer terms in a negative yield curve market. Banks can still attract such funding by paying a positive interest rate, but this will impact NIM severely when even long- dated government bonds are paying a negative yield;"
  > "loan product behaviouralisation: there is reduced incentive for customers to repay loans on maturity but instead to keep rolling them over;"
  > "for IRR purposes, floating rate loans and deposits can be placed in the '1- month' tenor bucket to match tenors."
  > — Chapter 5, line 4846 ^choudhry-neg-4

- Kỷ luật ALM không đổi khi lãi suất âm, nhưng hành vi khách hàng đổi; tác động lớn nhất với ngân hàng không chuyển lãi âm sang tiền gửi khách hàng.
  > "ALM discipline does not change in principle in a negative rates environment, but of course customer behaviour does and this must be addressed. The impact is greatest for banks that do not pass on the negative rates to customer deposit balances."
  > — Chapter 5, line 4850 ^choudhry-neg-5

- Truyền thống ALM là quản lý rủi ro thanh khoản và rủi ro lãi suất; "Strategic ALM" quản lý toàn bộ tài sản và nợ từ một góc nhìn tích hợp.
  > "Traditionally 'ALM' meant managing liquidity risk and interest- rate risk. But this isn't full 'ALM' if what one wishes to manage is all the assets and all the liabilities from one integrated, coherent aggregate viewpoint."
  > — Chapter 5, line 4856 ^choudhry-salm-2

- Bộ ba Treasurer, CFO, CRO, thông qua ALCO, cần có ảnh hưởng lớn hơn tới origination và định giá khách hàng để giải bài toán "3D": tuân thủ quy định, tăng NIM, hài lòng khách hàng.
  > "In the Basel III era, in order to meet the 3D optimisation challenge faced by banks one must seek to achieve maximum balance sheet efficiency, and that calls for the risk 'triumvirate' of Treasurer, CFO and CRO, operating through ALCO, to have a bigger influence in origination and customer pricing."
  > "Without this approach, it will be difficult to optimise the asset- liability mix that addresses the '3D' problem of regulatory compliance, NIM enhancement and customer franchise satisfaction."
  > — Chapter 5, line 4866 ^choudhry-salm-3

- Khoảng 95% nhu cầu phòng ngừa FX của các ngân hàng có thể đáp ứng bằng sản phẩm tiền mặt hoặc phái sinh plain vanilla.
  > "It is safe to say that 95% of the world's banks' FX hedging requirements can be met with cash or plain vanilla derivative products."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5118 ^choudhry-fxs-1

- Cross-currency swap là giao dịch ngoại bảng trao đổi gốc và lãi bằng hai đồng tiền; khác IRS ở chỗ có trao đổi gốc; gốc được đổi lại khi đáo hạn theo cùng tỷ giá.
  > "A currency swap, also known as a cross- currency swap, is an offbalance sheet transaction in which two parties exchange principal and interest in different currencies. It is similar to an interest-rate swap, except that in a currency swap there is an exchange of principal, while in an interest-rate swap the principal does not change hands."
  > "On the maturity date, the parties exchange the same principal amounts, reversing the initial exchange at the same exchange rate. It is exactly this initial exchange of principle and reverse of the same amount that enables the customer bank to manage its FX risk and the related interest-rate risk in the foreign currency."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5122 ^choudhry-fxs-2

- Cross-currency swap cố định hoặc phòng ngừa phơi nhiễm tiền tệ và lãi suất, đổi lại chi phí cơ hội; chênh lệch tiền tệ nên nằm trong khẩu vị rủi ro structural FX (và lãi suất) do Board phê duyệt trong RAS.
  > "By entering into a currency swap transaction a bank is aiming to fix or hedge its currency and interest-rate exposures. This eliminates risk but incurs a potential opportunity cost - FX rates could move in favour of the bank with the translation exposure. However, the point of ALM discipline is to remove uncertainty and preserve the net interest margin (NIM)."
  > "any currency mismatches should be maintained within an institution's risk appetite for structural FX risk (and interest-rate risk), both of which should be approved by the Board in the risk appetite statement."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5128 ^choudhry-fxs-3

- Thách thức của phòng ngừa bằng currency swap: biên lãi tích luỹ bằng ngoại tệ; khoản vay ngoại tệ trả trước hạn hoặc không trả được thì swap vẫn phải thực hiện, chấm dứt sớm swap có thể tốn kém.
  > "Margin income on currency lending will, in the illustrated example, be accrued in USD."
  > "Early repayment of the currency loan: if the loan is to wholesale market counterparties, mark- to- market early repayment charges can be applied. However, if the lending is to retail market counterparties, where other conventions may apply (such as a charge of a fixed percentage of the outstanding balance), early termination of the currency swap could prove costly to the bank undertaking the hedging."
  > "Non- performance of the currency loan: in this situation the terms of the currency swap would still need to be honoured. As above, early termination of the currency swap could prove costly."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5136 ^choudhry-fxs-4

- Ví dụ 6.6: ChoudWest huy động GBP 50 triệu cố định 0,30%, cho vay USD 65 triệu thả nổi RFR + 2,0%, và vào cross-currency swap 2 năm nhận cố định GBP 0,20%, trả USD 6 tháng thả nổi, tỷ giá 1,30; thu nhập USD hằng năm 1,3 triệu.
  > "ChoudWest Bank has raised a 2- year GBP fixed rate deposit for £50 million at 0.30%, with interest payable every 6 months. Simultaneously, it has agreed to provide a USD 65 million bullet commercial loan at 6- month USD RFR + 2.0%. To hedge the interest rate and currency risk on the loan and deposit transactions, ChoudWest Bank enters into a 2- year receive fixed rate GBP at 0.20%, pay 6- month floating rate USD cross- currency swap. Exchange of principal under the swap occurs at a GBP/USD exchange rate of 1.30."
  > "Annual USD income to ChoudWest Bank is USD 1.3 million [(USD 910,000 - USD 260,000) × 2]"
  > "ChoudWest Bank may enter into FX forward contracts to protect the GBP value of the USD income"
  > — Chapter 6, line 5151 ^choudhry-fxs-5

- Đường cong dương: ngân hàng huy động ngắn, cho vay dài (maturity transformation, "funding short"); khoảng cách giữa hai kỳ hạn là "tail", tức interest-rate gap; lãi suất ngắn tăng làm thu hẹp biên lợi nhuận.
  > "When the yield curve is positively sloped, the conventional banking approach is to fund the book at the short end of the curve and lend at the long end. This is the very definition of banking and is known as maturity transformation ."
  > "This is known as creating a tail ; here the 'tail' is the gap between one week and three months - the interest- rate 'gap' that the bank is exposed to."
  > "In this case if short- dated interest rates rise, the bank may see its profit margin shrink or turn into a funding loss."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5183 ^choudhry-hedge-1

- Đường cong đảo ngược: ngân hàng (các yếu tố khác không đổi) cho vay ngắn, huy động dài ("funding long").
  > "In the case of an inverted yield curve, a bank will (all else being equal) lend at the short end of the curve and borrow at the longer end. This is known as funding long and is shown in Figure 6.8."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5191 ^choudhry-hedge-2

- Vay và cho vay lệch kỳ hạn tạo rủi ro lãi suất mà ngân hàng thường phòng ngừa; phòng ngừa tốt nhất là giao dịch đối ứng chính xác, giúp giảm basis risk nhưng ăn vào biên lợi nhuận.
  > "Borrowing and lending cash of mismatched tenors creates interest- rate risk exposure. In a banking book business, generally banks look to hedge this risk exposure. There is more than one way to do this. The best hedge for any trade is an exact offsetting trade. This is not always possible, nor indeed always desirable as it will eat into the profit margin. However, the advantage of a similar offsetting trade is that it reduces basis risk exposure and essentially locks in a profit."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5201 ^choudhry-hedge-3

- Gap kỳ hạn tương lai có thể phòng ngừa bằng interest-rate futures (short sterling 90 ngày, Eurodollar, Euribor); có thể dùng strip futures cho cả kỳ gap.
  > "A forward- term interest- rate gap exposure can be hedged using interest-rate futures. In the sterling market the instrument will typically be the 90- day short sterling future traded on the London International Financial Futures and Options Exchange (LIFFE). The equivalents in USD and EUR are the Eurodollar and Euribor contracts. A strip of futures can be used to hedge the term gap."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5207 ^choudhry-hedge-4

- Short-term interest-rate futures cố định lãi suất trên một khoản tiền gửi danh nghĩa cho một kỳ tương lai; là công cụ ngoại bảng; mua hợp đồng tương đương gửi tiền danh nghĩa, bán tương đương vay danh nghĩa.
  > "Short- term interest-rate futures fix the rate of interest on a notional fixed term deposit of money for a specified period in the future. The sum is notional because no actual sum of money is deposited when buying or selling futures; the instrument is off- balance sheet. Buying such a contract is equivalent to making a notional deposit, while selling a contract is equivalent to borrowing a notional sum."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5209 ^choudhry-hedge-5

- Ví dụ 6.8: phòng ngừa nhu cầu vay 6 tháng GBP 30 triệu cần 60 hợp đồng (mỗi hợp đồng GBP 500.000, kỳ 3 tháng): bán 30 hợp đồng tháng 9 và 30 hợp đồng tháng 12, gọi là strip hedge.
  > "In order to hedge a 6- month GBP 30 million exposure the dealer needs to use a total of 60 futures contracts, as each has a nominal value of GBP 500,000, and corresponds to a 3- month notional deposit period. The dealer decides to sell 30 September futures contracts and 30 December futures contracts, which is referred to as a strip hedge."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5266 ^choudhry-hedge-6

#### Ghi chú convert u10
- Toàn bộ công thức FX cross-rate, forward rate, swap points, futures (Eq. 6.1–6.3, ví dụ 6.2–6.8) bị mất (`<!-- formula-not-decoded -->`); không trích.
- Figures 5.12, 5.13, 6.2–6.8 là ảnh, không có dữ liệu.

### Unit u11 — Ch6 Balance Sheet Management (phần tiếp: FRA, IRS, PVBP, OIS, option hedge, bond futures, securitisation, Treasury investment guidelines)

- Ví dụ 6.8 (tiếp): strip hedge đạt lãi suất vay thực 6,5325%, gần strip rate mục tiêu 6,53%; dealer vẫn chịu basis risk khi mua lại hợp đồng tháng 12 lúc hợp đồng tháng 9 đáo hạn.
  > "In this case the hedge has proved effective because the dealer has realised a borrowing rate of 6.5325%, which is close to the target strip rate of 6.53%."
  > "The dealer is still exposed to basis risk when the December contracts are bought back from the market at the expiry date of the September contract."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5296 ^choudhry-hedge-7

- FRA là hợp đồng ngoại bảng: bên mua vay danh nghĩa, bên bán cho vay danh nghĩa một số tiền ở lãi suất cố định cho một kỳ tương lai; vào ngày fixing, chênh lệch giữa lãi suất hợp đồng và lãi suất thực tế được thanh toán và chiết khấu về đầu kỳ.
  > "Under an FRA a buyer agrees notionally to borrow and a seller to lend a specified notional amount at a fixed rate for a specified period; the contract to commence on an agreed date in the future. On this date (the 'fixing date') the actual rate is taken and, according to its position versus the original trade rate, the borrower or lender will receive an interest payment on the notional sum equal to the difference between the trade rate and the actual rate. The sum paid over is present- valued as it is transferred at the start of the notional loan period, whereas in a cash market trade interest would be handed over at the end of the loan period."
  > "In hedging an interest- rate gap in the cash period, the trader will buy an FRA contract that equates to the term gap for a nominal amount equal to their exposure in the cash market."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5302 ^choudhry-hedge-8

- Ví dụ 6.9: ngân hàng cần vay GBP 10 triệu sau 3 tháng trong 12 tháng, mua FRA 3×15 ở 5½%; khi Libor lên 6%, khoản thanh toán FRA bù phần chi phí vay tăng.
  > "Treasury decides to buy a 3×15 ('threes- fifteens') FRA to cover the 12- month period beginning 3 months from now. A dealer bank quotes 5½% for the FRA, which the company buys for a notional GBP 10 million."
  > "The FRA has enabled the bank to lock in funding rates today for a forward- starting transaction."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5312 ^choudhry-hedge-9

- IRS plain vanilla là thoả thuận ngoại bảng trao đổi lãi định kỳ trên notional: một bên trả cố định, một bên trả thả nổi theo chỉ số thị trường; không trao đổi gốc.
  > "An interest- rate swap is an off- balance sheet agreement between two parties to make periodic interest payments to each other. Payments occur on a predetermined set of dates in the future, based on a notional principal amount; one party is the fixed- rate payer, the rate agreed at the start of the swap, and the other party is the floating- rate payer, the floating rate being determined during the life of the swap by reference to a specific market rate or index. There is no exchange of principal, only of the interest payments on this principal amount."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5316 ^choudhry-hedge-10

- Ứng dụng swap chia hai loại: asset-linked swap (đổi đặc tính dòng thu nhập của tài sản) và liability-linked swap (đổi cơ sở lãi suất của nợ).
  > "Swap applications can be viewed as being one of two main types: assetlinked swaps and liability- linked swaps. Asset- linked swaps are created when the swap is linked to an asset such as a bond in order to change the characteristics of the income stream for investors. Liability- linked swaps are traded when borrowers of funds wish to change the interest basis of their liabilities."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5328 ^choudhry-hedge-11

- Swap là công cụ phòng ngừa hiệu quả vì có convexity dương; thước đo rủi ro chính khi phòng ngừa bằng swap là PVBP/DV01, dùng để tính hedge ratio.
  > "However, swaps are particularly efficient instruments to use because they display positive convexity characteristics; that is, the increase in value of a swap for a fall in interest rates exceeds the loss in value with a similar magnitude rise in rates."
  > "The primary risk measure we require when hedging using a swap is its 'present value of a basis point' or 'dollar value of 01 [basis point]' PVBP/ DV01."
  > "The PVBP measure is used to calculate the hedge ratio when hedging a bond position."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5356 ^choudhry-hedge-12

- Ví dụ swap 5 năm, notional £1 triệu: PVBP của swap £425, thấp hơn trái phiếu cố định 5 năm £488,45 do PVBP của chân thả nổi (£63,45); quy tắc kinh nghiệm: PVBP của swap xấp xỉ PVBP trái phiếu cố định có kỳ hạn từ ngày reset kế tiếp tới đáo hạn.
  > "The swap BPV is lower than that of the 5- year fixed- coupon bond; that is, £425 compared to £488.45. This is because of the impact of the floatingrate bond risk measure, which reduces the risk exposure of the swap as a whole by £63.45. As a rough rule of thumb, the PVBP of a swap is approximately equal to that of a fixed- rate bond that has a maturity similar to the period from the next coupon reset date of the swap through to the maturity date of the swap."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5424 ^choudhry-hedge-13

- Hedge ratio theo PVBP giả định đường cong dịch chuyển song song nên tạo sai số phòng ngừa đáng kể theo thời gian; khắc phục bằng dynamic hedging (convexity adjustments).
  > "This technique is still used in the market but suffers from the assumption of parallel yield- curve shifts and will lead to significant hedging error over time. This is addressed through the application of dynamic hedging, which refers to constant adjustments to the hedge during the life of the transaction. These adjustments are sometimes referred to as convexity adjustments ."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5440 ^choudhry-hedge-14

- OIS trả cố định đổi lấy lãi suất qua đêm; ở thị trường sterling gọi là SONIA swap, đồng euro gọi là €STR.
  > "Overnight index swaps (OIS) are interest- rate swaps that are traded in the money markets. Conventional swaps pay fixed against a quarterly or semiannual floating payment. OIS swaps pay fixed against the overnight interest rate. In the sterling market they are known as sterling overnight interest- rate average swaps, or SONIA, while euro- currency OIS are known as €STR."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5444 ^choudhry-hedge-15

- SONIA swap trao đổi lãi suất cố định lấy trung bình nhân của lãi suất qua đêm trong kỳ hợp đồng, trao đổi lãi hằng quý.
  > "A SONIA swap is a swap contract that exchanges a fixed interest rate (the swap rate) against the geometric average of the overnight interest rates that have been recorded during the life of the contract. Exchange of interest takes place on a quarterly basis as per a conventional interest- rate swap."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5448 ^choudhry-hedge-16

- Lãi suất cố định của SONIA swap phản ánh lãi suất qua đêm kỳ vọng; SONIA tương quan chặt với lãi suất cơ bản của BoE; vì vậy SONIA swap có thể phòng ngừa NII khi cho vay cố định được tài trợ bằng tiền gửi lãi suất biến đổi, vốn không gắn chính thức với chỉ số tham chiếu.
  > "The fixed rate quoted on a SONIA swap represents the average level of the overnight interest rates expected by market participants over the life of the swap. The SONIA rate is very closely correlated with the Bank of England's base rate. As such, a SONIA swap can be used to hedge NII that arises when fixed rate loans are funded with variable rate deposits. Customer deposits, be they retail or corporate customers, are not often linked explicitly to reference rates like SONIA; instead they are set informally in line with market rates, and supply and demand."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5454 ^choudhry-hedge-17

- Ví dụ 6.11: cho vay dài hạn cố định tạo rủi ro NII nếu chi phí tiền gửi (đi theo nhưng không gắn chính thức với lãi suất cơ bản NHTW) tăng; nguồn coi đây là một dạng basis risk; ngân hàng có thể vào OIS.
  > "Long- dated loans are often originated at a fixed interest rate to meet customer requirements. From a first- order perspective, this gives rise to NII risk if the cost of the bank's deposits, which follow (but do not link explicitly to) central bank base rate, rises. This may be seen as a form of 'basis risk' interest- rate risk. To hedge this risk, the bank may enter into an OIS swap."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5460 ^choudhry-hedge-18

- Trong phần lớn trường hợp, FRA, futures và swap là đủ để phòng ngừa rủi ro lãi suất; trong một số trường hợp, ALM desk có thể muốn dùng option.
  > "We note that FRAs, futures and swaps can be used to hedge such exposure, and the flexibility and liquidity offered by these instruments means that in most cases they are all that is needed when hedging interest- rate risk. However, in certain cases a bank ALM desk may want to use option products."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5470 ^choudhry-hedge-19

- Ngân hàng dùng nhiều nợ thả nổi để tài trợ tài sản cố định là "liability-sensitive": lãi suất tăng làm NII và NIM giảm; rủi ro này có thể phòng ngừa bằng interest-rate cap. Tài sản tái định giá chậm hơn nợ cũng tạo rủi ro khi lãi suất tăng, tác giả gọi là "secondary IRR", một dạng basis risk.
  > "A bank that made extensive use of floating- rate liabilities in its funding, particularly if funding fixed- rate assets, is exposed to the risk of lower NII in the event that interest rates rise. It would be described as a 'liability- sensitive' bank, because as interest rates rose its funding cost would increase, while its asset returns may not."
  > "This risk exposure can be hedged by using an interest- rate cap, essentially a put option on interest rates, to reduce the extent of the risk exposure."
  > "Even if all the assets are not fixed rate, as indeed many would not be, they may still re- price on a less frequent basis than the liabilities and this also creates interest- rate risk in a rising rate environment, what the author terms 'secondary IRR' and a form of basis risk."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5472 ^choudhry-hedge-20

- Ví dụ 6.12 (ngân hàng bán lẻ giả định, bảng cân đối 200 triệu USD): gap tức thời −80; lãi suất +1% song song làm NII giảm 800.000 USD, tương đương khoảng 7,8% EAR hoặc NIM giảm 40bp. NIM định nghĩa là chênh lệch thu nhập lãi và chi phí lãi trên tài sản sinh lời bình quân.
  > "This liability sensitivity is indicated by the 'instant' gap of -80.00, as shown in the table."
  > "We observe that if there is a 1% upward parallel shift in rates, NII is reduced by USD 800,000.00. Thus the bank is carrying currently approximately 7.8% of EAR exposure. Put another way, the risk represents a 40 bps reduction in the NIM. We define NIM as the dollar difference between interest income and interest expenses, expressed as a percentage of average earning assets."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5478 ^choudhry-hedge-21

  Table 6.2 (gap report giản lược, triệu USD) và Table 6.3 (kịch bản NII):

  | Khoản mục | Số dư | Lãi suất |
  |---|---|---|
  | Loans: fixed rate | 140 | 8% |
  | Bonds: fixed rate | 40 | 7% |
  | Bonds: floating rate | 20 | 5% |
  | Risk-sensitive assets (RSA) | 20 | |
  | Deposits: floating rate | 100 | 3% |
  | Deposits: fixed rate | 60 | 3% |
  | NIBLs | 20 | 0% |
  | Capital | 20 | 0% |
  | Risk-sensitive liabilities (RSL) | 100 | |
  | Gap | −80,0 | |
  | RSA/RSL | 20% | |

  | | Không đổi | −1% song song | +1% song song |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | NII | 10,2 | 11 | 9,4 |
  | NIM | 5,10% | 5,50% | 4,70% |
  | Earnings at risk | | | 7,84% |

- ALCO thường đặt hạn mức EAR ±5% hoặc ±10%; với ví dụ trên, hạn mức 5% bị vượt. Không có phái sinh thì ALM desk có thể giảm nợ thả nổi hoặc tăng tài sản thả nổi, nhưng điều chỉnh bằng sản phẩm tiền mặt không làm ngay được.
  > "It may be that this is acceptable to ALCO, although typically ALCO will set a ±5% or ±10% limit on EAR. For our hypothetical bank under the current scenario the latter would not present a problem; however, the former would."
  > "If derivatives are not available, the ALM desk could reduce the amount of floating- rate funding and increase fixed- rate funding; equally it could increase the amount of floating- rate assets."
  > "The problem with using cash assets to reduce EAR exposure is that they cannot be effected right away."
  > "By using derivatives the risk exposure can be adjusted immediately. An FRA can be used to cover the funding risk;"
  > — Chapter 6, line 5480 ^choudhry-hedge-22

- Mua cap notional 80 triệu USD (bằng funding gap chịu rủi ro) loại bỏ hoàn toàn EAR; tenor theo chân trời rủi ro (ví dụ cap 1 năm); strike thường at-the-market (3%); strike out-of-the-money (4%) rẻ hơn nhưng để lại một phần EAR.
  > "In this case, the bank can buy a cap of notional USD 80 million (the amount of the funding gap at risk), which would remove the EAR completely. A lower notional would leave some exposure, and a higher notional would change the interest- rate sensitivity of the bank to one that benefited from a rise in rates."
  > "Generally, the strike price of the option would be set at the prevailing interest rate (at- the- market), so if the current 3- month Libor rate is 3% then the cap strike would be set at 3%."
  > "For a lower option premium, the ALM desk may want to set a higher strike rate, say 4%, which is 'out- of- the- money' and leaves some residual EAR."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5525 ^choudhry-hedge-23

- So với swap hay FRA, option giữ được lợi khi lãi suất giảm và chỉ tốn phí một lần; swap hoặc FRA buộc ngân hàng trả tiền khi lãi suất giảm, và swap loại bỏ biến động thu nhập cả hai chiều. Trong ví dụ, chi phí phòng ngừa bằng 5bp NIM; sau phòng ngừa, NII 10,1 và NIM 5,05% ở cả kịch bản không đổi và +1%.
  > "If the hedge was constructed with an interest- rate swap or FRA, the bank would have to pay out on either of these instruments if rates moved lower."
  > "In other words, a swap removes earnings volatility in both a rising and a falling interest- rate environment, so while the risk protection is complete there is no chance of upside gain. With an option the bank has a chance to benefit from an upward move in rates. The cost of the hedge is the premium, which is of course paid irrespective of whether the option expires in- the- money or not. In our example, this hedge cost amounted to 5 bps in NIM terms."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5529 ^choudhry-hedge-24

- Ngân hàng "asset sensitive" làm ngược lại: mua floor, chi trả khi Libor lúc đáo hạn thấp hơn strike.
  > "Note that a bank that was 'asset sensitive' would do the opposite to what is described here, it would purchase a floor option that would pay out if the Libor rate on expiry was below the strike rate."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5531 ^choudhry-hedge-25

- ALM desk dùng government bond futures để phòng ngừa IRR của danh mục trái phiếu (hiếm khi cho danh mục cho vay); bond futures giao vật chất, dùng cho phơi nhiễm lãi suất dài hạn.
  > "A widely used risk- management instrument used by ALM desks to hedge the IRR of bond portfolios (rarely, if ever, loan portfolios) is the government bond futures contract."
  > "Unlike short- term interest- rate futures, which require only cash settlement, bond futures require the actual physical delivery of a bond when they are settled. They are used to hedge longer- dated interest- rate risk exposures, such as a portfolio of government bonds."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5554 ^choudhry-hedge-26

- Chênh lệch giữa giá tiền mặt và giá futures là basis; rủi ro basis thay đổi không dự đoán được là basis risk.
  > "The difference between the cash price and the futures price is known as the basis . The risk that the basis will change in an unpredictable way is known as basis risk."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5574 ^choudhry-hedge-27

- Securitisation chuyển tài sản có dòng tiền dự đoán được thành chứng khoán bán cho nhà đầu tư, thường qua SPV.
  > "Securitisation is a financial engineering technique that involves the conversion of certain types of assets with predictable cash flows into marketable securities. Generally speaking it is the process of creating securities backed by pools of assets that are then sold to institutional investors."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5614 ^choudhry-sec-1

- Ngân hàng securitise để huy động vốn dài hạn và đa dạng hoá funding, giải phóng bảng cân đối (có thể giảm vốn yêu cầu nếu có chuyển giao rủi ro tín dụng được công nhận), và tăng trưởng bảng cân đối nhanh hơn; với ALM, đây là công cụ cho dung lượng bảng cân đối và chuyển giao rủi ro tín dụng.
  > "as a means of raising long- term funding, from the capital markets, and thereby also diversifying their funding base;"
  > "freeing up balance sheet capacity by selling existing loans to the SPV depending on how the transaction is structured, this may also deliver a regulatory capital benefit (that is, lower their regulatory capital requirement) if recognised credit risk transfer has taken place;"
  > "From an ALM point of view then, securitisation can be a key tool addressing balance sheet capacity and possible credit risk transfer (and allied regulatory capital relief) requirements."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5618 ^choudhry-sec-2

- Hai cấu trúc phát hành phổ biến: static pool/pass-through (pool cố định, notes amortise theo tài sản) và revolving pool/master trust (có thể thêm tài sản mới và phát hành nhiều lần).
  > "Issuance structure: a variety of issuance structures exist to accommodate different types of asset , the most common of which are static pool/ pass- through and revolving pool/master- trust."
  > "In a static pool transaction, the asset pool is fixed, with no substitution."
  > "New assets, meeting eligibility requirements, may be transferred to the trust to replace redeemed assets and/or to enable new issuance."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5663 ^choudhry-sec-3

- Securitisation thả nổi thường tài trợ bằng notes theo spread trên IBOR 3 tháng; nếu tài sản gắn chỉ số khác thì gap risk phải được phòng ngừa, còn basis risk (ví dụ IBOR 3 tháng so với Bank Base Rate) bị tổ chức xếp hạng đánh giá tiêu cực nếu không giảm thiểu bằng swap.
  > "Swap provider: a floating- rate term securitisation is almost always financed by notes issued at a spread to 3- month IBOR (or its equivalent in other jurisdictions). There will be an interest-rate risk to manage in the structure if the assets are linked to any other benchmark rate, for example, 5- year fixed rate, Bank Base Rate, Standard Variable Mortgage Rate and so on. Gap risk must be hedged and basis risk, for example between 3- month IBOR on the notes and Bank Base Rate on a securitisation of mortgage assets will be looked upon negatively by rating agencies unless it is mitigated by swaps."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5709 ^choudhry-sec-4

- Subordination bảo vệ tranche cao hạng bằng tranche thấp hạng; theo luật EU, originator phải giữ lại 5% ABS phát hành.
  > "Subordination - enables highly rated (senior) tranches to be protected from loss of principal and interest by lower rated (junior) tranches. Under EU legislation, originators must retain 5% of any issued ABS to align their interests with those of investors."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5738 ^choudhry-sec-5

- Sau Basel III, hầu như mọi ngân hàng luôn thặng dư nợ phải trả trong điều kiện BAU; ngân hàng có thể có chính sách đầu tư cho hai danh mục: "HQLA book" theo Basel III và danh mục non-HQLA nhưng vẫn thanh khoản.
  > "In the post- Basel III era, pretty much every bank in the world will always be surplus liabilities, as a matter of course, during BAU times."
  > "In some cases, to enhance return on the liquidity book, a bank may wish to have an investment guidelines policy for two portfolios, one being the 'HQLA' book that adheres to Basel III guidelines and a second portfolio that holds securities that are non- HQLA eligible but are nevertheless often still liquid instruments (such as short- term and medium- term securities issued by banks)."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5748 ^choudhry-tig-1

- Trong mẫu chính sách đầu tư Treasury của ChoudWest, quy mô tối thiểu của HQLA book do khẩu vị rủi ro stress thanh khoản trong RAS và khung stress test ILAAP quyết định, hướng dẫn bởi LCR và Survival Days.
  > "The minimum size of the HQLA will be given by the risk appetite for liquidity stress as stated in the bank's RAS, and within the ILAAP stress testing framework. The guidance is provided by two metrics, the LCR and Survival Days."
  > — Chapter 6, line 5794 ^choudhry-tig-2

  Mẫu phân bổ HQLA book (Example 6.14, Portfolio A):

  | Strategy | Rating | Min | Max |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | Money Markets book (deposits) tenor 1–24 tháng | > BBB+ | 30% | 100% |
  | Sovereign Bonds | AA | 0% | 50% |
  | Multilateral Agencies | AAA | 5% | 50% |
  | Tier 1 Bank Covered Bonds Senior Tranche | AAA | 0% | 20% |

#### Ghi chú convert u11
- Công thức PVBP (Eq. 6.4–6.7), hedge ratio, invoice amount, conversion factor hedge ratio bị mất; không trích.
- Table 6.1 (PVBP swap) bị vỡ thành các dòng rời; chỉ trích câu kết luận £425 / £488,45.
- Table 6.4: dòng "Payments from Cap" ghi `0.8 [80mm * (5% - 4%)]`, trong khi lời văn nói strike 3% và lãi suất thả nổi lên 4% ở kịch bản +1%. Giá trị 0,8 khớp với 80 × (4% − 3%); chuỗi diễn giải trong ngoặc của bảng có thể sai trong bản gốc. Không trích dòng này.

### Unit u14 — Ch9 ALCO (phần cuối: PRA SS1/13, ToR, mô hình quản trị, MI pack, basis risk page) & Ch10 ALM Sundries (asset encumbrance)

- ALCO là uỷ ban quản lý cấp cao và uỷ ban rủi ro quan trọng nhất của ngân hàng; phải có thành phần đúng, MI cập nhật và tranh luận thực chất.
  > "The ALCO is the most important senior management and risk committee in a bank. It must always be run as a value- added committee, which means it needs to have the correct composition, review up- todate management information that reflects accurately the balance sheet position and risk exposures of the bank and be the centre of robust discussion and debate."
  > — Chapter 9, line 6897 ^choudhry-alco-4

- FSA ban hành hướng dẫn good practice về ALCO tháng 1/2011 (thư "Dear CEO"); PRA tái ban hành nguyên nội dung thành Supervisory Statement SS1/13 tháng 4/2013.
  > "Financial Services Authority, Finalised Guidance: Asset and Liability Management , January 2011. This was a 'Dear CEO' letter issued by the FSA. When the Prudential Regulatory Authority (PRA) was created in 2013 and took over from the FSA, it reissued this letter as a Supervisory Statement, SS1/13, in April 2013. There was no change to the content, but a SS carries formal weight and legislative authority, technically (although practically perhaps not) stronger than a Dear CEO letter."
  > — Chapter 9, line 6911 ^choudhry-alco-5

- Vai trò ALCO theo FSA: kiểm soát chủ động theo chiến lược, tập trung toàn bộ bảng cân đối; giữ rủi ro trong khẩu vị; xét tác động lên biến động thu nhập; bảo đảm cơ chế định giá vốn nội bộ tính đúng chi phí thanh khoản; làm trọng tài giữa các khối kinh doanh.
  > "Proactively controls the business in line with the bank's strategy and objectives, and focuses on the entire balance sheet."
  > "Ensures that risks remain within the stated risk appetite."
  > "Considers the impact on earnings volatility of changing economic and market conditions."
  > "Ensures that an appropriate internal funds pricing mechanism is in place that correctly charges for the cost of liquidity, incentivises the desired behaviour and is in line with the bank's strategic objectives and risk appetite."
  > "Acts as the arbitrator in the debate and challenge process between business lines."
  > — Chapter 9, line 6915 ^choudhry-alco-6

- Thành phần theo FSA: CEO hoặc Phó CEO tham dự, CFO làm chủ tịch; gồm tất cả trưởng khối kinh doanh, CRO, trưởng nghiên cứu/kinh tế và trưởng kiểm toán nội bộ.
  > "Attended by the CEO or Deputy CEO, and chaired by the CFO;"
  > "Includes all business group heads, the CRO, the Head of Research/ Economics and the Head of Internal Audit."
  > — Chapter 9, line 6923 ^choudhry-alco-7

- Thảo luận tại ALCO theo FSA: hướng tới tương lai; ra quyết định chủ động hoặc báo cáo lên ExCo/Board thay vì chỉ ghi nhận rủi ro; có thể uỷ quyền cho tiểu ban; biên bản phải tóm tắt mức độ tranh luận, không chỉ ghi action point.
  > "Takes proactive decisions to manage ALM risks, act to solve issues raised or otherwise escalating to the Executive Committee/ Board rather than simply noting or observing the risks;"
  > "Considers recommendations from a tactical sub- committee that excludes the CEO and other ExCo members. Where appropriate, delegates decision- making authority to an ALCO sub- committee;"
  > "Provides minutes summarising the extent of discussion and debate, and do not only record action points."
  > — Chapter 9, line 6929 ^choudhry-alco-8

- MI pack của ALCO theo FSA: chỉ tiêu thanh khoản và funding theo đồng tiền kèm dự báo; kết quả stress test; phân tích rủi ro lãi suất bằng modified duration và VaR, NII/NIM sensitivity và basis risk; cơ cấu funding và điểm rủi ro tái tài trợ; kịch bản stress thanh khoản nhiều mức; CFP cập nhật định kỳ.
  > "Shows liquidity and funding metrics by currency; also provides a forecast of metrics based on current market expectations;"
  > "Provides analysis of interest- rate risk using modified duration and VaR methodologies, NII/NIM sensitivity and basis risk, as part of an assessment of earnings volatility;"
  > "Reports its current funding composition, and assesses potential refinancing risk stress points, based on its funding maturities, its market funding position and the position of the market generally;"
  > "Presents the Contingency Funding Plan (CFP) for the bank, and regularly updates it;"
  > — Chapter 9, line 6940 ^choudhry-alco-9

- Sau khủng hoảng 2007–2008, tối ưu hoá bảng cân đối khó hơn vì ba lý do: vốn và thanh khoản nhiều và đắt hơn; quy định can thiệp sâu hơn (phải xin phép supervisor cho thay đổi nhỏ về mô hình kinh doanh, sản phẩm); chú trọng hơn tới đối xử công bằng với khách hàng.
  > "This had a number of effects on banking operations, and it also made the objective of 'balance sheet optimisation' more difficult to achieve, for three main reasons: first, the greater amount and cost of the key resources of capital and liquidity, which acted as a constraint; secondly, more intrusive regulation meant that banks in many jurisdictions have to ask for approval from the supervisor before making seemingly minor changes to their business model and/or product suite; and thirdly, the greater emphasis on customer good conduct and treating customers fairly."
  > — Chapter 9, line 6954 ^choudhry-alco-10

- Trong các uỷ ban có trách nhiệm với rủi ro bảng cân đối (Board, Board risk, ExCo, risk committee, credit committee, ALCO), ALCO là uỷ ban gắn nhất với rủi ro bảng cân đối một cách tích hợp và chỉ sở hữu rủi ro bảng cân đối; vì vậy Board nên uỷ quyền cần thiết cho ALCO.
  > "The executive committee that is most closely concerned with balance sheet risk on a strategic and integrated basis (both sides of the balance sheet and all aspects of risk) is the asset-liability committee. More significantly, it has the bandwidth to undertake this role most effectively because that is all it does: it owns balance sheet risk and nothing else."
  > "Thus, from the Board's perspective the most effective way to ensure above- satisfactory and effective governance, stewardship and oversight of the balance sheet is to provide ALCO with requisite delegated authority from the Board to enable it to carry out its mission."
  > — Chapter 9, line 6971 ^choudhry-alco-11

- Trích ALCO ToR khuyến nghị của tác giả (Figure 9.5):
  > "ALCO operates under Board-delegated authority and reports direct to the Board."
  > "Three members, at least one of whom shall be the Chair or the Deputy Chair. If the meeting is quorate but there is no representative from the 2 nd Line of Defence, the CRO will be informed of decisions made at ALCO on meeting close."
  > "Monthly and ad hoc as required by any member."
  > — Chapter 9, line 6983 ^choudhry-alco-12

  | Mục ToR | Nội dung (Figure 9.5) |
  |---|---|
  | Chair | CFO; vắng CFO thì Head of Treasury (Deputy Chair) |
  | Members | CFO, CEO, CRO, Head of Treasury, Group Head of BSM (với group), trưởng các khối Corporate, Retail, Private, Wholesale Banking, Head of Strategy |
  | Authority | Board uỷ quyền, báo cáo trực tiếp Board |
  | Quorum | 3 thành viên, có Chair hoặc Deputy Chair |
  | Meeting frequency | Hằng tháng và đột xuất |
  | Voting | Đa số đơn giản |
  | Secretary | Treasury |

- So với khuyến nghị 10 năm trước, ALCO nay ngang hàng (pari passu) với ExCo và báo cáo trực tiếp Board; Credit Committee có quan hệ báo cáo "dotted line" tới ALCO để các chính sách tín dụng có tác động lớn lên bảng cân đối được ALCO rà soát.
  > "ALCO is now pari passu , to use the lawyer's expression, with the Executive Committee. This ensures that balance sheet resilience is given equal parity, but no greater, with profit and return on capital. It now reports direct to the Board, as the ExCo does."
  > "To ensure that ALCO is able to exercise its oversight responsibilities over the whole balance sheet, the Credit Committee is assigned a 'dotted line' reporting line to ALCO."
  > — Chapter 9, line 7014 ^choudhry-alco-13

- Trách nhiệm chính của ALCO: phê duyệt chính sách quản lý bảng cân đối; định hướng cấu trúc bảng cân đối; giám sát chính sách tín dụng; phê duyệt sản phẩm mới (uỷ quyền cho new products committee); hướng dẫn quản lý liquid asset buffer; bảo đảm tuân thủ yêu cầu về thanh khoản và vốn.
  > "ALCO is a committee with Board- delegated authority (or alternatively, operating as a sub- committee of the Board, in which case the chair is a Board non- executive director and not the CFO), responsible for management of the bank's balance sheet with respect to all aspects of balance sheet risk, regulatory capital requirement and liquidity."
  > "guides the management of the bank's liquid asset buffer;"
  > "ensuring the bank is compliant with all regulatory requirements on liquidity and capital."
  > "The asset- liability committee is mandated by executive authority to act as the primary risk committee responsible for balance sheet risk management."
  > — Chapter 9, line 7031 ^choudhry-alco-14

- Tiểu ban của ALCO có thể gồm Balance sheet management committee, Product pricing committee, Liquidity and funding group, ICLAAP committee; tác giả khuyến nghị lập ICLAAP committee làm diễn đàn chính cho hồ sơ ICAAP và ILAAP.
  > "The sub- committees may include:"
  > "The 'ICLAAP' committee, which the author recommends that banks set up, is so- called because it serves as the primary forum for guiding the capital adequacy and liquidity adequacy submissions processes to the regulatory authority."
  > — Chapter 9, line 7048 ^choudhry-alco-15

- ALCO ưu tiên tổ hợp khối lượng thấp, biên cao vì khối lượng thấp tiêu tốn ít vốn hơn; ALCO nhận báo cáo thu nhập theo khối kinh doanh, biên lãi suất cho vay trên cost of funds theo time bucket.
  > "The ALCO will receive an income breakdown report, split by business line."
  > "In general, the ALCO will prefer low volumes and high margin as a combination, because lower volumes consume less capital."
  > — Chapter 9, line 7094 ^choudhry-alco-16

- Ngân hàng đa quốc gia có công ty con ở nước ngoài nên tổ chức ALCO theo vùng hoặc theo pháp nhân, với Group ALCO (GALCO) ở cấp tập đoàn; không nên có cả hai tầng vùng và pháp nhân.
  > "A bank operating in a multinational environment, in which the overseas entities are subsidiaries or associated legal entities rather than branches, should organise its governance via regional or legal entity ALCOs. There is then a Group ALCO (GALCO) that reviews matters at Group level."
  > "either a regional or legal entity ALCO arrangement is sufficient, but there is little need for both."
  > — Chapter 9, line 7108 ^choudhry-alco-17

- ALCO pack phải trả lời "Có" cho các câu hỏi kiểm tra (ngắn gọn, dễ hiểu, thông điệp rõ, trong 10–12 slide đầu thấy được rủi ro hiện tại và dự kiến); nên rà soát hình thức pack ít nhất mỗi năm một lần.
  > "Does it give, within the first 10-12 slides (or pages), the latest and most complete balance sheet information in a way that enables the reader to ascertain quickly what the current balance sheet risks are? And what the expected balance sheet risks might be?"
  > "It is worth reviewing the format, layout, appearance and structure of the ALCO pack at least once a year to ensure that it can still reply 'Yes' to these questions."
  > — Chapter 9, line 7183 ^choudhry-alco-18

- Trang "Basis Risk" trong ALCO MI pack (Figure 9.18) phân loại tài sản và nợ theo cơ sở lãi suất: cố định theo kỳ còn lại, administered, Bank Base Rate, không chịu lãi; cột Mis-Match là chênh lệch tài sản trừ nợ.
  > "FIGURE 9.18 ALCO MI pack, Basis Risk page."
  > — Chapter 9, line 7217 ^choudhry-alco-19

  | Cơ sở lãi suất | Tài sản (£) | Lãi suất TS | Nợ (£) | Lãi suất nợ | Mis-Match (£) |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | Fixed more than 12-mths | 15.300 | 3,57% | 32.160 | 1,99% | −16.860 |
  | Fixed 6 to 12-mths | 6.090 | 7,71% | 300 | 1,50% | 5.790 |
  | Fixed 3 to 6-mths | 120 | 7,75% | – | 0,00% | 120 |
  | Fixed less than 3-mths | 26.980 | 0,47% | – | 0,98% | 26.980 |
  | Administered | – | 0,00% | 31.850 | 1,24% | −31.850 |
  | Bank Base Rate | 51.680 | 3,33% | – | 0,00% | 51.680 |
  | Non-Interest | 9.440 | 0,00% | 45.300 | 1,20% | −35.860 |
  | Total | 109.610 | | 109.610 | | |

- Yêu cầu tối thiểu cho quy trình quản trị rủi ro bảng cân đối là một khung quản trị ALCO hiệu quả.
  > "The minimum requirement for a sound balance sheet risk management process in a bank is an effective and fit- for- purpose ALCO governance framework and structure."
  > — Chapter 9, line 7241 ^choudhry-alco-20

- Asset encumbrance là việc tài sản ngân hàng chịu các thoả thuận tài sản bảo đảm, trao cho chủ nợ hoặc bên bảo lãnh quyền sở hữu tạm thời hoặc quyền ưu tiên trên tài sản đó.
  > "Asset encumbrance refers to the existence of bank assets subject to 'financial collateral arrangements', giving creditors or guarantors of an institution's obligations, temporary legal title or certain preferential rights over such assets."
  > — Chapter 10, line 7264 ^choudhry-enc-1

- Ngân hàng encumber tài sản trong hoạt động thường ngày: repo, phát hành ABS hoặc covered bond; tài sản thường bị encumber gồm tiền mặt, chứng khoán nợ và vốn, khoản vay thế chấp, vay mua ô tô.
  > "Banks use collateral arrangements, and therefore encumber assets, in the normal course of business. The repo market is a market in secured borrowing, and assets given up as collateral in a repo become encumbered. Similarly, by issuing asset- backed securities or covered bonds, banks are able to reduce their cost of financing and diversify their funding sources, but the transaction again encumbers assets."
  > — Chapter 10, line 7270 ^choudhry-enc-2

- Overcollateralisation: tài sản bảo đảm có giá trị cao hơn khoản phơi nhiễm, thường qua haircut hoặc margin ratio.
  > "Another important feature of encumbrance is 'overcollateralisation', which means that the collateral provided by the posting party is of higher value than the underlying exposure that the collateral is securing. This is usually undertaken by means of a 'haircut' or 'margin ratio',"
  > — Chapter 10, line 7276 ^choudhry-enc-3

- Structural subordination: do haircut, một phần tài sản mà chủ nợ có bảo đảm được ưu tiên lại do chủ nợ không bảo đảm tài trợ; khi ngân hàng mất khả năng thanh toán, chủ nợ không bảo đảm và người gửi tiền ở vị thế xấu hơn.
  > "The haircuts applied to the collateral mean that part of the assets, to which secured creditors have preferential access, are financed by unsecured creditors."
  > "Since unsecured creditors become 'subordinated' to secured creditors, this effect is known as 'structural subordination'."
  > — Chapter 10, line 7288 ^choudhry-enc-4

- Contingent encumbrance là phần encumbrance tăng thêm khi stress (giá tài sản bảo đảm giảm, haircut tăng, margin thêm), có thể tạo vòng phản hồi bất lợi tới khi ngân hàng hết tài sản đủ điều kiện.
  > "The overall additional encumbrance required under stress is known as 'contingent encumbrance'. Given that under stress creditors generally prefer to lend on a secured basis and against high quality collateral, higher levels of contingent encumbrance may result in an adverse feedback loop until the bank runs out of eligible collateral, with no further capacity to issue secured funding"
  > — Chapter 10, line 7294 ^choudhry-enc-5

- Tác giả coi asset encumbrance là chỉ số "gone concern" (chỉ thực sự quan trọng khi vỡ nợ), khác các chỉ số "going concern" như LCR hay tỷ lệ cho vay/tiền gửi.
  > "This illustrates how asset encumbrance is not quite like the other ALMrelated risk metrics that are reported in an ALCO pack, because the level of encumbrance only really becomes a serious issue in the event of default."
  > "For that reason, the author prefers to think of asset encumbrance as a 'gone concern' risk metric, rather than a 'going concern' one like LCR or loan- deposit ratio."
  > — Chapter 10, line 7296 ^choudhry-enc-6

- Encumbrance cao có thể làm giảm khả năng huy động thêm hoặc làm tăng chi phí funding; ALCO cần nắm rõ mức encumbrance.
  > "High levels of asset encumbrance may reduce a bank's ability to obtain additional funding, or require it to pay more for funding, especially if there is limited transparency, given the uncertainty faced by unsecured creditors when pricing unsecured debt. Therefore it is important that ALCO has a clear picture of the bank's encumbrance level."
  > — Chapter 10, line 7336 ^choudhry-enc-7

- Thước đo thường là các biến thể của asset encumbrance ratio (tài sản bị encumber / tổng tài sản); các tỷ lệ này có hạn chế: tài sản không encumber có thể không dùng được, khác biệt theo mô hình kinh doanh, không phản ánh structural subordination.
  > "These are variations of an 'asset encumbrance ratio', which is calculated as the ratio of encumbered assets to total assets. These types of measures have some drawbacks. Some assets may not be realisable under insolvency, and others, despite being unencumbered, may not be available for encumbrance due to economic or legal restrictions."
  > "In addition, these ratios do not provide information on the structural subordination of unsecured creditors resulting from overcollateralisation."
  > — Chapter 10, line 7338 ^choudhry-enc-8

  Table 10.1 Asset encumbrance ratios:

  | Tỷ lệ |
  |---|
  | Encumbered Assets / Total Assets |
  | Encumbered assets and off-balance sheet collateral / Total assets and off-balance sheet collateral |
  | Encumbered Assets / Assets available for encumbrance |
  | Unsecured liabilities / Total unencumbered assets |
  | Unsecured liabilities / Assets available for encumbrance |
  | Unsecured debt excluding retail deposits / Assets available for encumbrance |

- LCR và NSFR của Basel III ưu đãi funding có bảo đảm và covered bond; một số cơ quan quản lý (Australia, Canada, Singapore) đặt giới hạn asset encumbrance.
  > "The Basel III liquidity metrics Liquidity Coverage Ratio (LCR) and Net Stable Funding Ratio (NSFR) favour secured funding and provide preferential"
  > "treatment for both issuance and holdings of covered bonds."
  > "Australia, Canada and Singapore have all set limits on asset encumbrance,"
  > — Chapter 10, line 7346 ^choudhry-enc-9

- Chính sách ALM của ngân hàng nên bao gồm cách xử lý và khẩu vị đối với asset encumbrance, kể cả đặt hạn mức encumbrance.
  > "A bank's ALM policy should include the treatment and appetite for asset encumbrance of the bank. This will include setting encumbrance limits."
  > — Chapter 10, line 7370 ^choudhry-enc-10

Ghi chú u14: phần Ch10 (asset encumbrance) tiếp tục sang u15 (danh sách nội dung chính sách encumbrance, hedge accounting, IFRS 9, EIR); u14 chỉ chứa tới dòng 7371.

### Unit u03 — Ch1 (phần giữa: money markets, reference rates, loan pricing, rủi ro chính, Example 1.5 case NII/NIM: base và Scenario 1)

- Lãi suất khách hàng gắn với đường cong qua reference rate; thường bằng lãi suất thị trường tiền tệ chuẩn cộng biên bù rủi ro tín dụng, liquidity premium, chi phí vận hành và lợi nhuận. Ví dụ: SONIA (UK), SOFR (Mỹ).
  > "A reference rate is a market standard interest rate that can be used to calculate the interest payable or receivable on a financial instrument, or used to determine another interest rate. Typically, the customer rate on a loan will be a standard money market interest rate plus a margin to cover credit risk, a liquidity premium, operating expenses and a profit margin for the bank. Examples of reference rates include SONIA (Sterling Overnight Index Average) in the UK and SOFR (Secured Overnight Financing Rate) in the USA, which may be averaged over a period, such as 3 months, in a financial contract."
  > — Chapter 1, line 1245 ^choudhry-ref-1

- Ngân hàng cũng dùng lãi suất quản lý nội bộ làm tham chiếu (lãi suất cơ bản NHTW, standard variable mortgage rate); SVR gắn với lãi suất cơ bản NHTW nhưng là lãi suất "administered" do ngân hàng tự đặt.
  > "Banks also use internal managed rate benchmarks as reference rates. Common examples in the UK include central bank base rates and standard variable mortgage rates. The latter is an interest rate basis that is linked explicitly to the central bank base rate but is an internal or 'administered' rate that is set by the bank itself."
  > — Chapter 1, line 1249 ^choudhry-ref-2

- Lãi suất trung bình qua đêm được ghép lãi hằng ngày, nên tiền lãi của kỳ chỉ biết chắc ngay trước ngày trả lãi. SOFR thay USD Libor (qua đêm, có bảo đảm bằng trái phiếu kho bạc Mỹ); SONIA thay GBP Libor.
  > "The key point to understand with overnight index average interest rates is that they compound every day, so the interest paid for a specified period where the rate is an overnight RFR is not known for certainty until just before the interest payment date."
  > "The replacement for USD Libor is the Secured Overnight Financing Rate (SOFR), which is an overnight interest rate based on overnight loans that are secured by US Treasury securities."
  > "The replacement for GBP Libor is sterling overnight index average (SONIA) which is an overnight rate that is based on the average rate of overnight money market transactions conducted the day before."
  > — Chapter 1, line 1251 ^choudhry-ref-3

- Định giá khoản vay nên dựa trên nguyên tắc tài chính doanh nghiệp, dù phần lớn ngân hàng là "price taker"; RoE mục tiêu dẫn dắt định giá, và mọi hoạt động phải tạo lợi nhuận vượt RoE mục tiêu.
  > "Setting the interest rate on a customer loan should follow, in the first instance, a traditional approach to valuation that is based on orthodox corporate finance principles. That said, most banks are 'price takers' in the sense that they need to be setting interest rates - for loans as well as deposits - in line with their peer banks otherwise they may struggle to attract customer business."
  > "Thus the target RoE, together with the other variables introduced in the previous section, drives loan pricing."
  > "In other words, the business must generate a return that exceeds the target RoE."
  > — Chapter 1, line 1281 ^choudhry-lp-1

- Ví dụ 1.1: lãi suất khoản vay hoà vốn 5,75% (hiện giá doanh thu sau thuế bằng vốn 10 dành cho khoản vay); biên hoà vốn 75bp trên lãi suất funding 5%; lãi suất phải đặt trên 5,75% mới tạo giá trị.
  > "Rearranging for X we obtain an interest rate of 5.75%. The interpretation of this is as follows: by setting an interest rate of 5.75%, the present value of the revenue earned on the loan, after tax, is equal to 10, which is the capital set aside for the loan."
  > "Therefore the loan interest rate must be set above 5.75%. At this rate or below, there is zero value creation."
  > "Note that the break- even loan rate of 5.75% is 75 bps (basis points) above the funding rate of 5%. This is the break- even margin."
  > — Chapter 1, line 1311 ^choudhry-lp-2

- Lãi suất hoà vốn là hàm của lãi suất funding, biên hoà vốn, RoE và thuế suất; tài sản không cần vốn rủi ro (trái phiếu chính phủ AAA) có thể có biên thấp hơn; lượng vốn cần thực tế phụ thuộc trọng số rủi ro của tài sản.
  > "Following naturally from this illustration in Example 1.1, we see that a bank should calculate the break- even interest rate charge on business as a function of its funding rate, the break- even margin, as well as its RoE and the corporate tax rate."
  > "For business lines that do not require any risk capital to support them, for example AAA- rated government bonds, the margin can be lower."
  > "In reality, the amount of capital required will depend on the 'risk weighting' of the asset (loan)."
  > — Chapter 1, line 1317 ^choudhry-lp-3

- Ví dụ 1.2: khi tính xác suất vỡ nợ 5% ở năm thứ hai và tỷ lệ thu hồi 40%, lãi suất hoà vốn của khoản vay hai năm là 11,1%; xác suất vỡ nợ và tỷ lệ thu hồi là giả định, đưa yếu tố chủ quan vào định giá.
  > "Rearranging for X we obtain an interest rate of 11.1%. This is the break- even loan rate that must be applied to the loan."
  > "Should default occur, the bank will recover 40 cents on the dollar."
  > "From this point on, we have introduced an element of subjectivity in the calculation: the recovery rate is an assumed value (we have no firm idea what we will recover in the event of a customer going into bankruptcy) and the default probability of any customer can never be known with certainty,"
  > — Chapter 1, line 1350 ^choudhry-lp-4

- Lãi suất cho vay đặt bằng spread trên chi phí funding, là hàm của RoE mục tiêu, credit spread bù tổn thất dự kiến và spread bù chi phí vận hành; chi phí duy trì HQLA có thể được tính ở nơi khác, không nhất thiết trong mô hình định giá khoản vay.
  > "The interest rate on a loan is then set as a spread over the bank's funding cost, being calculated as a function of the target RoE, a credit spread to cover anticipated loan losses, and any additional spread to cover its operating expenses."
  > "(for example, the cost of maintaining part of the balance sheet assets as risk- free sovereign securities - the so- called high quality liquid assets buffer or HQLA - can be covered elsewhere and not necessarily in the customer loan pricing model)"
  > — Chapter 1, line 1384 ^choudhry-lp-5

- Mô hình định giá của ChoudWest (Ví dụ 1.4) còn dùng để xác định lợi nhuận sản phẩm, đánh giá chi phí phòng ngừa và bảo đảm TLP được tính vào giá; mô hình có "Target Rate of Return Floor Rate (TRR)" để bảo đảm ngưỡng chi phí vốn, và ALCO được Board uỷ quyền phê duyệt khoản cho vay dưới mức này.
  > "As well as enabling loan pricing, the model is set such that the bank may determine specific product profitability, assess hedging costs and ensure that the term liquidity premium (TLP) is accounted for in pricing."
  > "The model incorporates the bank's 'Target Rate of Return Floor Rate (TRR)', which acts as a control mechanism to ensure that the cost of capital hurdle is observed for all lending. The bank's assetliability committee (ALCO) is delegated by the Board to authorise any lending below this rate."
  > — Chapter 1, line 1424 ^choudhry-lp-6

- Rủi ro thị trường là rủi ro lỗ do biến động giá trên thị trường tài chính (FX, lãi suất, basis); trong ngân hàng, rủi ro thị trường chính là rủi ro lãi suất, đôi khi đo và báo cáo riêng.
  > "This is risk of loss arising from movements in prices in financial markets. Examples include FX risk, interest- rate risk and basis risk. In banks the key market risk is interest- rate risk, and it is sometimes measured and reported separately to other market risk."
  > — Chapter 1, line 1500 ^choudhry-risk-1

- Rủi ro thanh khoản có hai nghĩa: với Treasury là rủi ro không đủ funding đáp ứng cam kết (rollover risk); với trader là rủi ro thị trường của tài sản trở nên quá mỏng.
  > "This refers to two different but related issues: for a Treasury or money markets person, it is the risk that a bank has insufficient funding to meet commitments as they arise. That is, the risk that funds cannot be raised in the market as and when required. This is also known as rollover risk, for obvious reasons."
  > "For a securities or derivatives trader, liquidity risk is the risk that the market for assets becomes too thin to enable fair and efficient trading to take place."
  > — Chapter 1, line 1504 ^choudhry-risk-2

- Ví dụ 1.5 (ChoudWest Bank giả định): lãi suất chung 5%, giữ nguyên 12 tháng; bảng cân đối không đổi 12 tháng, không trả nợ trước hạn, không nghiệp vụ mới; không có FTP; đường cong phẳng.
  > "For the purposes of the example, the general level of interest rates is assumed to be 5% and it is anticipated that they will remain at this level for the next 12 months."
  > "Each item on the balance sheet is expected to remain constant for the next 12 months. There is assumed to be no repayment or prepayment on fixed rate products, nor any new business written"
  > "ChoudWest Bank does not operate a funds transfer pricing process, as this could alter the recognition of interest income and expense across assets and liabilities."
  > "As a further simplification, the yield curve is assumed to be flat across all tenors."
  > — Chapter 1, line 1532 ^choudhry-nim-1

- Trường hợp cơ sở: thu nhập lãi £972,5m; nợ xấu ròng dự phòng vẫn tính vào tài sản sinh lãi để NIM phản ánh đúng; tài sản sinh lãi £16,0bn; chi phí lãi £511,5m; NII = £461,0m; NIM = NII / tài sản sinh lãi = 2,88%.
  > "Total interest income is £972.5m."
  > "Bad debts, net of provisions, are included in the calculation because items in this category are capable of earning interest."
  > "If non- performing assets were to be excluded, we would not be getting a true picture of profitability."
  > "Interest earning assets comprise all assets capable of earning interest (£16.0bn)."
  > "Net interest income (NII) is calculated by subtracting interest expense from interest income (£972.5m - £511.5m = £461.0m)."
  > "Net interest margin (NIM) is generated by dividing NII by interest earning assets (£461.0m/£16.0bn = 2.88%)."
  > — Chapter 1, line 1572 ^choudhry-nim-2

- Khi lãi suất chung đổi, lãi suất sản phẩm phản ứng theo ba cách: giữ nguyên (sản phẩm cố định, không chịu lãi); đổi bằng mức thay đổi benchmark (gắn hợp đồng với SONIA hoặc Base Rate của ngân hàng); đổi nhiều hơn hoặc ít hơn (thẻ tín dụng tái định giá ít nhưng mạnh; tài khoản tiền gửi lãi thấp cũ đổi ít hơn).
  > "The interest rate attached to the product would remain unchanged . This will be the case for fixed rate products (such as personal loans, fixed rate mortgages, fixed rate deposits and fixed rate subordinated liabilities) and zero- rate products (such as non- interest- bearing current accounts and bad debts) on both the asset and liability side of the balance sheet."
  > "The interest rate on the product would increase by the same amount as the underlying change in the general level of interest rates ."
  > "The interest rate on the product would increase by either more or less than the underlying change in the general level of interest rates . Products with a high interest rate, such as credit cards, will tend to re- price infrequently, but may re- price by much more than the underlying benchmark when there is a movement in short- term market interest rates or Base Rate."
  > — Chapter 1, line 1633 ^choudhry-nim-3

- Kịch bản 1 (lãi suất +3%): £250m khoản vay chuyển sang nợ xấu; thu nhập lãi £1.256,2m (+£283,7m), chi phí lãi £730,5m (+£219,0m), NII £525,7m (+£64,7m, +14%); NIM tăng 0,41 điểm % lên 3,29%, vì nhiều tài sản tái định giá hơn nợ.
  > "In this example, there is a migration of £250m of performing customer loans and advances to the non- performing or bad debt category."
  > "Total interest income is £1,256.2m (up £283.7m) and interest expense is £730.5m (up £219.0m), resulting in an increase in NII to £525.7m (up £64.7m)."
  > "In this case, the NII increases by £64.7m or 14% to a 3% upward movement in the general level of interest rates. In aggregate, more of the assets are re- pricing than the liabilities, which is helping to generate the income growth. The greater the change, the greater the level of interest-rate risk being run by ChoudWest Bank."
  > "As interest earning assets remain constant at £16.0bn , and NII has risen, the NIM also increases (by 0.41% to 3.29% )."

### Unit u04 — Ch1 phần cuối (Example 1.5 Scenario 2–5, NII/NIM sensitivity, quản lý NIM, dự báo NII/NIM, tóm tắt chương) & đầu Ch2

Kết quả: thu nhập lãi giữ £972,5m, chi phí lãi £542,5m (+£31,0m), tài sản sinh lãi £16,5bn, NII £430,0m (−£31,0m), NIM 2,61% (−0,28 điểm %).
  > "Scenario 4 involves the simultaneous launch of a new 0% credit card (for balance transfers), designed to increase ChoudWest Bank's market share and fee income, and the relaunch of an instant access deposit account, with a more attractive rate (up to 3.50% from 2.50%)."
  > "Interest earning assets are increasing, due to the launch of the new 0% credit card account, but this is using up balance sheet resources without delivering additional interest income."
  > "In aggregate, in common with many initiatives designed to boost market share, ChoudWest suffers from both an NII and NIM perspective."
